Theorem 37.1src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: IsWeilPair.mul
Let $p , m , m' \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime. Let $X$ be a group. Let $s , t \in X$. Let $j \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {X}{\GpMACRoFEY {p}}$ such that $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (s)}{m}$ and $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (t)}{m'}$. Then $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (s \times t)}{m + m'}$.
Weil pairs multiply: if (s, m) and (t, m') are Weil pairs, then so is (st, m + m').
Proof. By IsFrob.mul_iff, IsWeilPair. ∎
Theorem 37.2src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: IsWeilPair.pow
Let $p , m , n \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime. Let $X$ be a group. Let $s \in X$. Let $j \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {X}{\GpMACRoFEY {p}}$ such that $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (s)}{m}$. Then $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (s ^ {n})}{n \times m}$.
Proof. By IsFrob, IsFrob.pow
and 1 lemma of Mathlib pow_mul'
. ∎
Theorem 37.3src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: IsWeilPair.eq_pow_of_isFrob
Let $p , m , q \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime. Let $X$ be a group. Let $s \in X$. Let $j \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {X}{\GpMACRoFEY {p}}$ such that $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {j (s)}{m}$ and $\IsFrobMACRoBIB {\intQpBarMACRoEBB {p}}{q}{\InvinvMACRoFW {j (s)}}$. Then $q = p ^ {m}$.
If (s, m) is a Weil pair and s⁻¹ acts as x ↦ x^q modulo the maximal ideal of the valuation ring of Q̄_p, then q = p^m; in particular m = 0 if q = 1.
Proof. By valuation_natCast_eq_one, IsFrob.cycChar_eq
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Nat.prime_def, Nat.exists_infinite_primes, Fact.out
. ∎
Definition 37.4src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.wittToQpBar
Let $p \in \Natural$. Let $x \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Then $\LiftwittToQpBarMACRoCBG$ is an algebra homomorphism of $\LiftWittFqMACRoCAR {x}$ and the set of fixed algebraic closures of $p$ defined as $\mathrm{Exists\_\_choose} (\mathrm{Lift\_\_wittToQpBar\_\_\_proof\_9})$.
The Z_p-algebra homomorphism from Λ = W(F_{p^f}) to Q̄_p that becomes the restriction of θ after the inclusion of Q̄_p in C_p.
Theorem 37.5src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.coe_wittToQpBar
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $x \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Let $c \in \LiftWittFqMACRoCAR {x}$. Then the coercion of $\mathrm{Lift\_\_wittToQpBar\_\_app} (c)$ is equal to $\mathrm{theta\_\_app} (\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {c})$.
Proof. By exists_algHom_lift. ∎

Theorem 37.7src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.smul_witt_eq_iff
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $x \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Let $g \in \GpMACRoFEY {p}$. Let $c \in \LiftWittFqMACRoCAR {x}$. Then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {g}{\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {c}}$ is equal to $\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {c}$, if and only if $g (\mathrm{Lift\_\_wittToQpBar\_\_app} (c))$ is equal to $\mathrm{Lift\_\_wittToQpBar\_\_app} (c)$.
Proof. By Lift.coe_wittToQpBar, Lift.wittToQpBar_injective, Lift.WittFq.exists_smul_algebraMap_eq, theta_smul
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib Subtype.val_injective, WittVector.map_injective, UniformSpace.Completion.coe_inj
. ∎
Definition 37.8src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.FrobGroup
Let $p \in \Natural$ such that $\GroupOverGpMACRoAUP {p}$. Then $\LiftFrobGroupMACRoBVR {\Gamma}$ is a set defined as $\ProdMACRoEZ {\mathrm{Multiplicative} (\Integer)}{\GroupOverGpGMACRoAUQ}$.
The group ℤ × Γ (ℤ written multiplicatively); on rings with bijective Frobenius φ, such as A_inf, the pair (n, g) acts as φ^n j(g).

Theorem 37.18src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.isUnit_teichmuller_sub
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $f \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Let $a \in \SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\LiftflatFqMACRoCAY {f}}{x_{4}})}$. Then $\mathrm{WittVector\_\_teichmuller\_\_app} (p , a)- \mathrm{WittVector\_\_teichmuller\_\_app} (p , b)$ is a unit.
Proof. By Ainf.isUnit_iff_isUnit_constantCoeff
and 1 lemma of Mathlib map_sub
. ∎

Theorem 37.20src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.exists_flatFq_root
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $f \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Let $K$ be a field. Let $s \in \RingHomMACRoHO {\LiftWittFqMACRoCAR {f}}{K}$. Let $x_{1} \in K$ such that if $x_{1} \neq 0$, then the exponentiation of $x_{1}$ to the difference between the exponentiation of $p$ to the value of $f$ and $1$ is equal to $1$. Then there exists an element $a$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{4}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\LiftflatFqMACRoCAY {f}}{x_{4}})}$, such that $s (\mathrm{WittVector\_\_teichmuller\_\_app} (p , a))$ is equal to $x_{1}$.
Let k be the field of p^f elements in O^♭ and s: W(k) → K a ring homomorphism to a field. Every x in K with x^{p^f} = x is s([a]) for the Teichmüller lift [a] of some a in k.
Proof. By Lift.isUnit_teichmuller_sub
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.prim, by_cases, Function.mtr, IsPrimitiveRoot.map_of_injective, IsUnit.map, IsPrimitiveRoot.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one
. ∎
Theorem 37.21src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.fixes_roots_iff_fixesLambda
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $f \in \PNatMACRoEA$. Let $g \in \GpMACRoFEY {p}$. Then for all fixed algebraic closures $\zeta$ of $p$, if the exponentiation of $\zeta$ to the difference between the exponentiation of $p$ to the value of $f$ and $1$ is equal to $1$, then $g (\zeta)$ is equal to $\zeta$, if and only if $\LiftFixesLambdaMACRoBVQ {f}{g}$.
Proof. By Lift.exists_flatFq_root, Lift.smul_witt_eq_iff
and 3 lemmas of Mathlib NeZero.one_le, pow_sub₀, WittVector.ext
. ∎
Theorem 37.22src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.exists_smul_teichmuller_eq
Let $p , e \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime. Let $C$ be a closed subgroup of $\GpMACRoFEY {p}$. Let $\pi$ be a fixed algebraic closure of $p$ such that for all fixed algebraic closures $x_{1}$ and $y$ of $p$, for all elements $n$ of $\Natural$, if $0$ is less than $n$, then if $x_{1} ^ {n}$ is equal to $\pi$, then if $y ^ {n}$ is equal to $x_{1} ^ {n}$, then there exists an element $g_{1}$ of $\GpMACRoFEY {p}$, such that $g_{1}$ is an element of $C$ and $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {g_{1}}{x_{1}}$ is equal to $y$. Let $u , v \in \OFlatMACRoFET$. Assume that $\NeZeroMACRoGF {e}$. Assume that $\mathrm{PreTilt\_\_untilt\_\_app} (u)^ {e}$ is equal to the coercion of $\pi$. Assume that $\mathrm{PreTilt\_\_untilt\_\_app} (v)^ {e}$ is equal to $\mathrm{PreTilt\_\_untilt\_\_app} (u)^ {e}$. Then there exists an element $g$ of $\GpMACRoFEY {p}$, such that $g$ is an element of $C$ and $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {g}{\mathrm{WittVector\_\_teichmuller\_\_app} (p , u)}$ is equal to $\mathrm{WittVector\_\_teichmuller\_\_app} (p , v)$.
Let C be a closed subgroup of G_p that is transitive on the n-th roots of π for every n > 0. If u, v in O^♭ have untilts with e-th power π, e > 0, then some g in C sends the Teichmüller lift [u] to [v].
Proof. By isClosed_setOf_smul, OFlat.untiltRoot, smul_coe_Cp, OFlat.mk_untiltRoot, coeff_tiltMap, IsAIC.exists_root_map_eq
and 12 lemmas of Mathlib WittVector.map_teichmuller, Function.iterate_succ', Polynomial.monic_X_pow_sub_C, IsCompact.nonempty_iInter_of_sequence_nonempty_isCompact_isClosed, map_pow, pow_mul, Perfection.ext, pow_one, IsClosed.isCompact, PreTilt.untilt_iterate_frobeniusEquiv_symm_pow, NeZero.pos, IsClosed.inter
. ∎
Class 37.23src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.KummerChart
Let $p \in \Natural$ such that $\GroupOverGpMACRoAUP {p}$. Then $\LiftKummerChartMACRoBWH {\Gamma}$ is a proposition.
A Kummer chart: a chart and a subgroup Γ' of index prime to p, containing the elements near 1, with j(Γ') the fixer of Λ and θ(z), containing for each l ≥ 0 a g with gz = [ε]^l z.

Definition 37.33src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.KummerChart.IsUnramified

The Kummer chart is unramified: E = z + p, the index e is 1, and d = 0.

Theorem 37.35src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChartExists.lean: Lift.exists_kummer_chart
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group such that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Then there exists an element $k$ of $\mathrm{Lift\_\_KummerChart\_\_on} (p , \GroupOverGpmkMACRoAUS {X})$, such that for all elements $g$ of $X$, $\LiftChartFixesThetaZMACRoBUO {\LiftKummerChartchartMACRoBZI}{\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g)}$, $\LiftCharteMACRoBUX {\LiftKummerChartchartMACRoBZI}\times \mathrm{PNat\_\_val} (\LiftChartfMACRoBVA {\LiftKummerChartchartMACRoBZI})$ is equal to the index of the range of $\OverGptoGpMACRoCFX$, if $\inertiaGroupMACRoEAO {\GpMACRoFEY {p}}{\intQpBarMACRoEBB {p}}\leq \mathrm{MonoidHom\_\_range} (\OverGptoGpMACRoCFX)$, then $\LiftKummerChartIsUnramifiedMACRoBWM$ and there exists an element $\phi$ of $\GpMACRoFEY {p}$, such that $\phi$ is an element of the range of $\OverGptoGpMACRoCFX$ and $\IsFrobMACRoBIB {\intQpBarMACRoEBB {p}}{p ^ {\mathrm{PNat\_\_val} (\LiftChartfMACRoBVA {\LiftKummerChartchartMACRoBZI})}}{\phi}$.

Lib.PadicHodge.WachKisin.KummerChart

Theorem 37.36src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachKisin/KummerChart.lean: Lift.Chart.IsChartOf.dvd_extendToBPlus
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group such that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $c \in \LiftChartMACRoBUM$ such that $\LiftChartIsChartOfMACRoBUP {c}{\OverGptoGpMACRoCFX}$. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $a$ be a function from elements of $I$ to fixed algebraic closures of $p$ such that for all elements $l_{2}$ of $I$, $a (l_{2})$ is an element of $\localFieldOfMACRoEFI {X}$. Let $f$ be a linear map of $\AinfMACRoFEU$ and $\AinfMACRoFEU$. Let $y$ be a function from $I$ to $\BPlusMACRoNQ$. Theorem 37.59src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/RegularityFrobenius.lean: exists_retraction

Proof. By Function.Injective.of_ker, Bcris
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib Module.Flat.lTensor_preserves_injective_linearMap, LinearMap.ker_eq_bot, TensorProduct.tmul_eq_smul_one_tmul, LinearMap.leftInverse_apply_of_inj
. ∎
Theorem 37.60src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/RegularityFrobenius.lean: fontaine_regularity
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group such that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $F$ be a filter on $X$ such that $\nearOneMACRoEIB {X}\leq F$. Let $I$ be a set. Let $b \in \BcrisMACRoON$. Let $w \in \FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$. Assume that $b \neq 0$. Assume that $\FilterEventually {g_{1}}{\EqtMACRoDE {(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {b}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g_{1})}{w})})}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g_{1})}{b})}{w})}}{F}$. Then there exists an element $a$ of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$, such that $w$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {b}{a}$ and $\FilterEventually {g}{\EqtMACRoDE {(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g)}{a})}{a}}{F}$.
Fontaine's regularity: let j have open image and every set of F contain the preimage of a neighbourhood of 1. If b ≠ 0 in B, w ∈ B^(ι) and b·gw = (gb)·w along F, then w = b·a with a ∈ B^(ι) fixed along F.
Proof. By BPlus, nhds_one_le_map_nearOne, BPlus.isUnit_p, Bcris.exists_witt_ratio
and 10 lemmas of Mathlib Finsupp.range_mapRange, smul_eq_zero_iff_eq, IsUnit.dvd_mul_left, Eq.ge, mul_left_comm, IsUnit.pow, MulZeroClass.mul_zero, Filter.Eventually.mono, IsUnit.map, Finsupp.ext_iff
. ∎
Theorem 37.61src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/RegularityFrobenius.lean: le_of_fixed_mem_of_saturated
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group such that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $F$ be a filter on $X$ such that $\nearOneMACRoEIB {X}\leq F$. Let $I$ be a set. Let $W$ and $W'$ be submodules of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$ over $\BcrisMACRoON$ such that for all $b \neq 0$ in $\BcrisMACRoON$, for all elements $w_{1}$ of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W}{\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {b}{w_{1}}}$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W}{w_{1}}$, for all elements $v$ of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W}{v}$, then $\FilterEventually {g_{1}}{\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g_{1})}{v})}}{F}$ and for all elements $w$ of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {I}{\BcrisMACRoON}$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W}{w}$, then if $\FilterEventually {g}{\EqtMACRoDE {(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g)}{w})}{w}}{F}$, then $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {W'}{w}$. Then $W \leq W'$.
Elimination. Let j have open image, F be coarser than 'near 1', and W ⊂ B^(ι) a saturated submodule, each v in W having j(g)v in W for F-almost all g. Then the vectors of W fixed by F-almost all g span W.
Proof. By le_of_echelon, fontaine_regularity
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib left_eq_mul₀, Finsupp.smul_apply, mul_eq_zero, Filter.Eventually.mono, smul_eq_mul, Finsupp.ne_iff
. ∎

Theorem 37.67src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/RegularityFrobenius.lean: linearFrob_mul

Then $\linearFrobMACRoEEM {\rho}{(s \times t)}{(m + k)}$ is equal to $\linearFrobMACRoEEM {\rho}{s}{m}\times \linearFrobMACRoEEM {\rho}{t}{k}$.
Multiplicativity of the linear Frobenius: for j with open image, T potentially crystalline, and Weil pairs (j(s), m) and (j(t), k), one has β_{st, m+k} = β_{s,m} β_{t,k}.

. ∎

Theorem 37.90src/Lib/PadicHodge/Comparison/CrystallineLimit.lean: isCrystalline_of_trace_limit

\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ Theorem 37.128src/Lib/PadicHodge/Limit/LocalLimit.lean: local_clauses_of_trace_limit
Let $x \in \Natural$ such that $x$ is prime and $\PadicIsoMACRoCGT {x}$. Let $E$ be a field. Let $m$ be a finite type. Let $I$ be a set. Assume that $\EmbeddedNumberFieldMACRoAJH {E}$. Let $r \in \GalRepMACRoARQ {x}{E}{m}$. Let $A \in \ClosurePlaceMACRoQL$. Assume that $r$ is continuous. Assume that $\IsTraceNondegMACRoBPD {r}$. Let $R$ and $R'$ be functions from elements of $\Natural$ and elements of $\Natural$ to propositions. Let $J$ be a function from elements of $\RingHomMACRoHO {E}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$ to multisets of $\Integer$. Let $\iota$ be a function from elements of $I$ to sets such that for all elements $\eta_{10}$ of $I$, $\iota (\eta_{10})$ is a finite type. Let $F$ be a filter on $I$ such that $F$ is a proper filter. Let $a$ be a function from elements $x_{23}$ of $I$ and elements of $\iota (x_{23})$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $J'$ be a function from elements $x_{16}$ of $I$ and elements of $\iota (x_{16})$ and elements of $\RingHomMACRoHO {E}{\ComplexMACRoAM}$ to multisets of $\Integer$. Theorem 37.137src/Lib/PadicHodge/Characters/UnramifiedPeriods.lean: exists_unramified_period
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $T$ be a commutative ring such that $T$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$, $\mathrm{Module\_\_Free} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, T)$ and $\mathrm{Module\_\_Finite} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, T)$. Let O be a finite free commutative Z_p-algebra, a ∈ O with 1 − a nilpotent mod p. Some unit y of A_inf⊗O has φ^n g·y ∈ O·y for all n ≥ 0, g ∈ G_p, and φ^k s·y = y, s·y = a^k y for Weil pairs (s, k).

Theorem 37.138src/Lib/PadicHodge/Characters/UnramifiedPeriods.lean: isPotCrys_of_unramified_char
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group. Let $T$ be a commutative ring. Assume that $X$ is a topological space. Assume that $T$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $\nu$ be a monoid homomorphism from $X$ to the set of units of the set of fixed algebraic closures of $p$. Assume that $T$ is an algebra over the set of fixed algebraic closures of $p$. Assume that $\OverGptoGpMACRoCFX$ is an open embedding. Assume that $\nu$ is continuous. Assume that $\FilterEventually {g_{1}}{\mathrm{if} (\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g_{1})}{0}, \EqtMACRoDE {\nu (g_{1})}{1})}{\nhdsMACRoGQ {1}}$. Let $\rho$ be a monoid homomorphism from $X$ to the set of ends of the set of fixed algebraic closures of $p$ and $T$ such that for all elements $g$ of $X$, for all elements $x_{6}$ of $T$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\rho}{g}}{\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\nu (g)}}{x_{6}}}$. Let j be an open embedding, ν: Γ → Q̄_p^× a continuous character trivial on inertia near 1, acting by scalars on a commutative Q̄_p-algebra T. Then T is potentially crystalline, has the jumps of the trivial action, and X − ν(s) kills (s, k) for Weil pairs (j(s), k). Known result: Characters with finite image on inertia are potentially crystalline of weight 0.
Proof. By IsFrob.mul_iff, IsPotCrys, IsWeilPair, OverGp.toGp, exists_unramified_period, IsLocalGalois.of_finite, nearOne_eq_nhds_one, Dpcris, residueCard_padic, IsLocalGalois.frobenius_spec, IsLocalGalois, Ainf, jump_twist_of_period, pow_le_norm_aeval, CoeffRing.dvd_iff_norm_le, exists_gp_eq_algHom, IsFrob.pow, linearFrob_apply, FrobGal, coefEnd, nearOne, eventually_nearOne_apply_eq, thetaBPlus, CoeffRing.eventually_mem, Bcris, CoeffRing, tensorAction
and 40 lemmas of Mathlib Finset.mul_sum, LinearMap.ker_eq_top, norm_pos_iff, Ne.pos, Subgroup.index_ne_zero_of_finite, continuous_subtype_val, LinearMap.ext, Units.continuous_val, Continuous.comp, Set.MapsTo.closure, dist_eq_norm', NonarchimedeanGroup.is_nonarchimedean, LinearMap.cancel_right, Units.mulRight_bijective, map_inv₀, Filter.Eventually.and, exists_pow_lt_of_lt_one, OpenSubgroup.mem_nhds_one, LT.lt.le, right_ne_zero_of_mul, SMulCommClass.symm, Finset.eventually_all, Metric.continuous_iff', zero_le_one, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one, Filter.Eventually.mono, Filter.Eventually.exists, InvMemClass.inv_mem, Filter.Eventually.mp, isClosed_eq, Submodule.mem_span_of_mem, by_contra, IsUnit.ne_zero, IsUnit.map, Submodule.ker_mkQ, TensorProduct.exists_finset, LT.lt.not_ge, Topology.IsInducing.isClosed_iff', Filter.mem_comap', one_pos

Theorem 37.140src/Lib/PadicHodge/Characters/LocalCharacters.lean: isFiniteTypeAt_char

Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $O$ be a commutative ring. Let $X$ be a group. Assume that $O$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Then $\LiftInClassMACRoBWD {F}{\BotbotMACRoE}{\lambda x_{7} . \SingletonsingletonMACRoEW {\NegnegMACRoFV {c}}}{L}$.
Proof. By hasJumps_of_cyclotomicGp_zpow, isAdmissible_of_cyclotomicGp_zpow
and 1 lemma of Mathlib Multiset.mem_replicate
. ∎
Theorem 37.232src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/PairingDefs.lean: Lift.jump_tateDual
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $O$ be a commutative ring. Let $X$ be a group. Assume that $O$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Theorem 37.236src/Lib/PadicHodge/Crystalline/PairingDefs.lean: Lift.exists_isKilledBy_tateDual_tensor
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $O$ be a commutative ring. Let $X$ be a group. Assume that $O$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $F$ be a filter on $X$. Let $K$ and $K'$ be functions from elements of $X$ and elements of $\PolynomialMACRoHH {O}$ to propositions. Let $U$ and $U'$ be functions from algebra homomorphisms of $O$ and the set of number complexes ['\'\\\'p-adic_Adj\\\'\''] of $p$ to finite sets of $\Integer$. Let $Y , L \in \LiftGalLatticeMACRoBVV {O}{X}$. Assume that $\mathrm{Module\_\_Finite} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, O)$. Assume that $\LiftInClassMACRoBWD {F}{K}{U}{Y}$. Assume that $\LiftInClassMACRoBWD {F}{K'}{U'}{L}$. Assume that $O$ is an integral domain. Let $s \in X$. Let $q , r \in \PolynomialMACRoHH {\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}}$. Assume that $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (s)}{0}$. Assume that $q$ is monic. Assume that $r$ is monic. Assume that $K (s , \PolynomialmapMACRoIO {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_algebraMap} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, O)}{q})$. Assume that $K' (s , \PolynomialmapMACRoIO {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_algebraMap} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, O)}{r})$. Then there exists a polynomial $e$ over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$, such that $e$ is monic, $\mathrm{Polynomial\_\_natDegree} (e)\leq \mathrm{Polynomial\_\_natDegree} (q)\times \mathrm{Polynomial\_\_natDegree} (r)$ and $\IsKilledByMACRoBJS {\LiftGalLatticeucMACRoBVX {\LiftGalLatticetateDualMACRoBWB {(\LiftGalLatticetensorMACRoBWC {Y}{L})}}}{\InvinvMACRoFW {s}}{0}{\PolynomialmapMACRoIO {\mathrm{Algebra\_\_algebraMap} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}, O)}{e}}$.
Let j(s) lie in inertia, Y and L be potentially crystalline, and monic q, r in Z_p[X] kill (s,0) on Y and L. Then a monic e in Z_p[X] of degree ≤ deg q · deg r kills (s⁻¹,0) on Hom(Y ⊗ L, O(1)).
Proof. By Lift.GalLattice, IsWeilPair.pow, linearFrob_pow, Lift.GalLattice.tensor, Lift.InClass, Lift.InClass.isPotCrys, Lift.GalLattice.ρ, linearFrob, IsWeilPair.inv, coefEnd, Lift.InClass.tensor, Lift.baseChangePairing, Lift.InClass.dual, linearFrob_baseChangePairing

. ∎

Lib.PadicHodge.BlochKato.BlochKato

Theorem 37.237src/Lib/PadicHodge/BlochKato/BlochKato.lean: rank_comap_eq_add
Let $R$ be a commutative ring. Let $T$ and $T'$ be additive commutative groups such that $T$ is a module over $R$. Assume that $R$ is an integral domain. Assume that $T'$ is a module over $R$. Let $e$ be a linear map of $T$ and $T'$. Let $N$ be a submodule of $T'$ over $R$. Then the rank of $R$ and $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\mathrm{Submodule\_\_comap} (e , N)}$ is equal to $\mathrm{Module\_\_rank} (R , \SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\MinminMACRoED {N}{\mathrm{LinearMap\_\_range} (e)})})+ \mathrm{Module\_\_rank} (R , \SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\mathrm{LinearMap\_\_ker} (e)})$.
Rank–nullity for preimages: for a linear map e of modules over a domain and a submodule N of T', rk e⁻¹(N) = rk(N ⊓ range e) + rk ker e.
Proof.
By 6 lemmas of Mathlib Submodule.map_comap_eq, inf_comm, LinearEquiv.rank_eq, LinearMap.range_domRestrict, LinearMap.rank_range_add_rank_ker, LinearMap.ker_le_comap
. ∎
Theorem 37.238src/Lib/PadicHodge/BlochKato/BlochKato.lean: crysCocycles.finrank_eq
Let $p$ be a prime natural number. Let $X$ be a group. Let $R$ be a commutative ring. Let $T$ be an additive commutative group. Assume that $R$ is an algebra over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $T$ is a module over $R$. Assume that $T$ is a module over $\PadicIntMACRoFDG {p}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{p}$. Let $\rho$ be a representation of $X$ and $T$. Bloch–Kato rank formula: if T is finite free over Z_p and potentially crystalline with multiset of jumps J over Z_p, then Z_f(T) is finitely generated over Z_p of rank rk T + [G_p : j(Γ)]·#{negative jumps in J}, counted with multiplicity. Proof. By reindex_equiv, thetaBdR, Bcris.fundamental_sequence_surjective, BdR.exists_sub_mem_of_smul_sub_mem, isHausdorff_of_injective_pi, OverGp.toGp, BdRPlus, qpBar_mem_hodgeFil_iff, BdR, Bcris.exists_padic_eq_of_frobEnd_fixed, finrank_fixedFieldOf, smul_algebraMap_eq_algebraMap_smul, rank_comap_eq_add, localFieldOf, mem_localFieldOf, actionRep, crysCocycles, equivFun_tensorAction, coordAction, FrobGal, free_finite_of_rank_le, IsPotCrys.exists_hodge_basis, overZp, Bcris, frobEnd, d0

Theorem 37.276src/Lib/PadicHodge/WachGamma/Letters.lean: Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.exists_span_formsOfType_mod_pred
Let $p \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime and $\GroupOverGpMACRoAUP {p}$. Assume that $\LiftKummerChartMACRoBWH {\Gamma}$. Let $O \in \LiftKummerChartCoeffsMACRoBWI$. Let $L \in \LiftKummerChartJumpLatticeMACRoBWN {O}$. Let $i \in \LiftKummerChartLetterMACRoBYH$. Let $u$ be an integer. Then there exists $r \leq \LiftKummerChartJumpLatticejumpMultiplicityMACRoBXJ {i}{u}$ in $\Natural$, such that there exists a function $\nu$ from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {r}$ to elements of $\LiftKummerChartJumpLatticeformsOfTypeMACRoBXB {i}{u}$, such that for all elements $\mu$ of $\LiftKummerChartJumpLatticeFunctionalMACRoBWP$, if $\mu$ is an element of $\LiftKummerChartJumpLatticeformsOfTypeMACRoBXB {i}{u}$, then there exists a function $c$ from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {r}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $p$, such that $\mu - \FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{m}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c (m)}{\nu (m)})}$ is an element of $\LiftKummerChartJumpLatticeformsOfTypeMACRoBXB {i}{(u - 1)}$.
Let O be a domain and L potentially crystalline. The forms of type u at the letter i (`Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.formsOfType`) are spanned over Q̄_p, modulo those of type u − 1, by at most Σ jump_γ(u) of them, summed over the γ with γ(ζ) = θ(ω(i)). Known result: Kisin modules: Galois action trivial modulo the variable, Hodge–Tate weights read off.
Proof. By Lift.Chart.f, Lift.KummerChart.Γ', Lift.DiagTensor.mk, thetaBdR, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.mulZeta, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.formsOfType, χ, OverGp.toGp, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.mem_letterPart_iff_mulZeta, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.inClass, forall_smul_eq_iff_mem_localFieldOf, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.Hom.wittMap_letterProj, Lift.KummerChart.nearOne_le, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.letterProj_mem_letterPart, Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice.comp_letterProj_mem_forms, Lift.InClass.isPotCrys, Lift.GalLattice.ρ, mul_mem_hodgeFil, smul_tUnit_zpow, Lift.GalLattice.Carrier, GroupOverGp.G, algebraMap_mem_hodgeFil_one_iff, IsPotCrys.sum_jump_eq_jump_overZp
